# Color Names

Color names are resolved in the context of a [color scheme](attributes/colorscheme.md). Graphviz currently supports the [X11 scheme](color-names.md#x11), the [SVG scheme](#svg), and the [Brewer schemes](#brewer), with X11 being the default.

Color names are case-insensitive.

In addtition to color names defined by schemes you can also use [custom colors](attribute-types/color.md).

The Brewer color schemes below are covered by this [license](#brewer_license).

* * *
